Wait, if the pawn at g2 moves, the Rook at c2 still has to get past the Bishop at e2.
So `e2f1` is essential.
After `e2f1`, the 2nd rank is open from c2 to h2.
If g2 is still there, `c2g2#`.
If g2 is gone, `c2h2#`.
Wait, if g2 is gone, the king is at h2, and the rook moves to h2, it's just capturing the king.

So `e2f1` is the winning move.
